HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B009","root_001307/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2af208188c1911692a73","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"14:8:4\",\"root_001307\",[\"B004\"]],[\"14:8:9\",\"root_000259\",[\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Nankörlüğün bütün yeryüzüne yayılması bile nimet ilişkisinin varlığını silemez.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3bd3bcee306c5d6c1741","text":"1. **The maximal coalition cannot transfer need to God.** `ج م ع B009` supplies wholeness without scattering or loss, and `غ ن ي B001` self-sufficiency. `أَنتُمْ وَمَن فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ جَمِيعًا` constructs the largest available earthly total; 14:9 then unfolds many generations without changing the divine asymmetry. The packet gives the totalization, and I infer the failed transfer: even complete collective covering creates no lack in God. `غَنِىّ` is thereby read relationally against the strongest imaginable coalition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B001","root_001110/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_43c9ddaadb0ac2b7b0ef","text":"1. **Universal refusal cannot create divine need.** `ج م ع` `B001` gathers scattered parts into a whole; against that maximum aggregate, `غ ن ي` `B001` supplies self-sufficiency and `ح م د` `B003` the praiseworthy one of many good traits. Later all people emerge together, yet each self is recompensed individually. I infer the universal counterfactual tests whether praise depends on an audience; it does not. The ayah reads as severing gratitude from any economy of divine gain.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4501f58b967c489c8424","text":"The collective exposure of 14:21 followed by individual recompense in 14:51 clarified that even total human aggregation cannot shift either divine sufficiency or personal responsibility.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5285ed15ccb0f8635bbf","text":"3. **Kufr is ingratitude scaled to the earth.** `ك ف ر B004` supplies covering over favor, sharpened by the immediate gratitude-kufr opposition in 14:7. The global subject in 14:8 scales that act without altering its kind. I infer that the verse tests whether cumulative nonrecognition can erase the benefaction relation; the answer supplied by `غَنِىّ حَمِيد` is no. The ayah reads as a limit-case for ingratitude rather than a hypothetical threat to divine standing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000355/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6928c262d3607255a097","text":"2. **`حَمِيد` carries both intrinsic praise and a legitimate claim grounded in favor.** `ح م د B001` supplies praise and thanksgiving, while `ح م د B005` supplies claiming praise through benefaction. 14:5-7 supply signs, favor, gratitude, and increase before this verse declares divine self-sufficiency. I infer two nonexclusive readings: God remains praiseworthy independently of response, and the prior benefaction gives a valid claim to acknowledgment without creating need. The adjective is therefore neither dependent on human praise nor disconnected from the favor sequence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000355/B001","root_000355/B005","root_001110/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9b4ba7d342be2c3ea72f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"14:8:12\",\"root_001110\",[\"B001\"]],[\"14:8:13\",\"root_000355\",[\"B001\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Allah'ın övgüye layık oluşu insan övgüsüne bağlı değildir; yine de önceki nimetler bu övgü için geçerli bir karşılık zemini oluşturur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_de83d403b895d0f20ea9","text":"14:9 expanded the single hypothetical total into generations of actual communities. That later historical spread made `جَمِيعًا` function as a maximal coalition test and confirmed that accumulated nonrecognition does not produce divine need.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
